- **ifixai**: Run an independent iFixAi audit of the user's deployed agent, free on their own machine or hosted on a paid workspace, testing whether it does the job it is supposed to do given their business rules and org structure, and whether it can be pushed outside them. You are the operator who finds the agent in their repo, confirms it, builds the simulation environment, connects its HTTP endpoint, previews the run, runs the audit and explains what it found. Use when the user asks to audit, inspect, red-team or stress-test an agent they have deployed, to read their iFixAi package, or to read a past ru… ([ifixai-ai/ifixai-plugin](https://github.com/ifixai-ai/ifixai-plugin/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/ifixai) · ★11)

- **ifly-mode-caveman**: Use when user has activated caveman mode via /ifly:mode caveman or when SessionStart injects mode=caveman. Apply to every subsequent natural-language response — maximally compressed English (no articles, no pronouns, no filler, telegram-style). Generated code stays idiomatic. ([ljn7/ifly](https://github.com/ljn7/ifly/tree/HEAD/skills/ifly-mode-caveman) · ★3)
- **ifly-mode-minimal**: Use when user has activated minimal mode via /ifly:mode minimal or when SessionStart injects mode=minimal from state. Apply to every subsequent response — one-line summaries, no bullet lists in prose, no markdown headers, code comments only where non-obvious. ([ljn7/ifly](https://github.com/ljn7/ifly/tree/HEAD/skills/ifly-mode-minimal) · ★3)
- **ifly-mode-normal**: Use when user has activated normal mode via /ifly:mode normal or when SessionStart injects mode=normal. No-op passthrough — behave as the base Claude Code system prompt would. ([ljn7/ifly](https://github.com/ljn7/ifly/tree/HEAD/skills/ifly-mode-normal) · ★3)
- **ifly-mode-silent**: Use when user has activated silent mode via /ifly:mode silent or when SessionStart injects mode=silent from state. Apply to every subsequent response in this session — no preamble, no summaries, output only the direct answer. ([ljn7/ifly](https://github.com/ljn7/ifly/tree/HEAD/skills/ifly-mode-silent) · ★3)
- **ifly-mode-verbose**: Use when user has activated verbose mode via /ifly:mode verbose or when SessionStart injects mode=verbose. Apply to every subsequent response — explain reasoning, surface alternatives considered, add inline documentation to generated code, end with a change summary. ([ljn7/ifly](https://github.com/ljn7/ifly/tree/HEAD/skills/ifly-mode-verbose) · ★3)
